\section*{exp\_\allowbreak{}crossworld\_\allowbreak{}robust\_\allowbreak{}controlvariate\_\allowbreak{}design}

\noindent\textit{Specialization:} v1\par

\paragraph{TL;DR.} An unpaired pilot identifies the two potential-outcome marginals but not their cross-world copula.  The note converts the sharp Fr\'echet covariance interval into an endpoint maximum for Zhao's affine residual-variance criterion, solves the regular rank-two problem with finite exact-input certificates, and treats Zhao's full all-rank pseudoinverse carrier through a measurable arbitrary-real selector and a separate exact-input cylindrical-algebraic selector.  A compact stable all-rank carrier makes observable deployment auditable: its regret relative to the unrestricted value is bounded by the exact stability price \(b_n^{\mathrm{st}}\), pilot error, optimization tolerance, and the selected-rank variance bridge.  The triangle witness proves an exact robust reduction of \(4912/39525\) in the basis objective and a positive observable variance gap for one displayed pair of tie representatives; no universal strictness or free stability is claimed.

\section{Project justification}

\paragraph{Gap.} Zhao's ex ante control-variate basis depends on a cross-world covariance that separate arm-specific pilot samples do not identify.  Existing sharp covariance bounds do not by themselves provide a globally certified basis optimizer, an all-rank pseudoinverse selector, or a bridge from the selected objective to the observable estimator under rank changes.

\paragraph{Niche.} The experimental network, exposure maps, and assignment law are fixed and known, while two independent unpaired pilot samples identify exactly the two bounded marginal laws.  This separates design randomness from the ex ante dependence-robust basis decision and makes the unidentified copula the sole ambiguity in the endpoint objective.

\paragraph{Fill.} The paper imports the sharp Fr\'echet covariance range, proves affine endpoint reduction, gives projective and rank-stratified certified optimization procedures, proves plug-in value and selector regret, and exposes a stable all-rank deployment carrier.  Unlike sharp adjusted-variance-bound results and optimized interference variance-estimation results, it jointly selects an all-rank control-variate basis against fixed-marginal copula ambiguity from an external unpaired pilot and bounds its observable deployment regret.  Its observable guarantee pays the explicit term \(b_n^{\mathrm{st}}=v_n^{\mathrm{st}}-v_{\mathrm{mm}}^+\) and a finite rank-adaptive bridge remainder rather than assuming singular-rank stability.

\section{Related work}

Zhao2026OptimalControlVariates supplies the network control-variate objective, the pseudoinverse convention, and the zero-covariance surrogate.  AronowGreenLee2014SharpVariance supplies the sharp Fr\'echet covariance endpoints and their attainment; Bergin1999RestrictedMarginals informs continuity of fixed-marginal coupling extrema.  MikhaeilGreen2024SharpRegressionAdjustment establishes sharp regression-adjusted variance-bound methodology, but does not select a pseudoinverse control-variate basis from unpaired marginal pilots under cross-world copula ambiguity or connect such an all-rank selection to observable-regret performance.  HarshawMiddletonSavje2026 optimizes variance estimation under interference and complex experimental designs; it does not deliver the fixed-assignment-law, cross-world endpoint basis-selection or rank-adaptive external-pilot deployment theorem proved here.  AronowSamii2017 supplies the exposure-mapping design foundation.  Real-closed-field quantifier elimination and cylindrical algebraic decomposition supply exact finite certificates on algebraically encoded inputs, while the countable dense selectors used for arbitrary real pilots are explicitly nonconstructive and have no finite stopping test.  CaiDeJanvrySadoulet2015 and Zhao's reanalysis motivate the unexecuted Jiangxi PICC protocol, but authenticated replication data are not available and no numerical application result is asserted.

\section{Setup}

Target (estimand / structural parameter): \(\tau=n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^n\{Y_i(1)-Y_i(0)\}\).
Primary functional / estimator / diagnostic: The causal estimand remains the fixed finite-population contrast \(\tau\), distinct from the ex ante basis criterion.  The unrestricted benchmark is \(v_{\mathrm{mm}}^+=\inf_{[B]_+\in\mathcal B_Z^+}w^+(B)\) on the note's quotient of Zhao's published all-rank pseudoinverse carrier.  For arbitrary real pilots, both the unrestricted and stable positive-tolerance selectors are classical pilot-measurable existence rules without finite stopping tests.  The stable carrier \(\mathcal K_n^{\mathrm{st}}\) includes the exact rank-zero Horvitz--Thompson fallback and spectrally separated rank-one and rank-two strata; its attained value satisfies \(v_n^{\mathrm{st}}=v_{\mathrm{mm}}^++b_n^{\mathrm{st}}\), where \(b_n^{\mathrm{st}}\ge0\) is the exact approximation-versus-stability price.  On exact algebraic empirical and population endpoint input, the stable CAD record certifies empirical \(\varepsilon_m\)-optimality, rank, coordinate envelope, positive-eigenvalue margins, and an \(\varepsilon_m\)-width enclosure of \(b_n^{\mathrm{st}}\).  Independent-pilot deployment through the displayed observable Horvitz--Thompson coefficient obeys the finite all-rank regret bound \( |V_{\mathrm{st}}-v_{\mathrm{mm}}^+|\le b_n^{\mathrm{st}}+2\mathbb E_Pd_m^++\mathbb E_P\varepsilon_m+\max_{r=1,2}R_{n,r}^{\mathrm{ad}}\).  The bridge remainder is zero at rank zero and follows at positive rank from Zhao's directed coordinate-versus-complement neighborhood condition only after the stable spectral and coordinate constraints are certified.  No rate or zero value for \(b_n^{\mathrm{st}}\), no arbitrary-real finite algorithm, and no agreement of selectors is claimed.  Bounded outcomes, an external unpaired pilot, pilot/main independence, and selected-rank stability remain explicit restrictions..
\begin{itemize}
  \item \texttt{n} --- \texttt{finite population size}; \(\{3,4,\ldots\}\); \texttt{finite-\allowbreak{}population index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The number of vertices in the fixed experimental network.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathbb A} --- \texttt{exposure-\allowbreak{}label set}; \(2^{\{0,1\}}\); \texttt{exposure index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The two exposure labels \(\mathbb A=\{1,0\}\).
  \item \texttt{a} --- \texttt{exposure label}; \(\mathbb A\); \texttt{index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} A generic label, with \(a=1\) denoting the focal exposure and \(a=0\) denoting the comparison exposure.
  \item \texttt{G} --- \texttt{known finite network}; \(\{0,1\}^{n\times n}\); \texttt{design primitive}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The fixed adjacency matrix on the \(n\) experimental units.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}nu} --- \texttt{known assignment law}; \(\mathcal P(\{0,1\}^n)\); \texttt{design primitive}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The randomization distribution chosen before outcomes are observed.}
  \item \texttt{W} --- \texttt{assignment vector}; \(\{0,1\}^n\); \texttt{only main-\allowbreak{}experiment randomness}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The treatment vector drawn from \(\nu\).
  \item \texttt{f\_\allowbreak{}i} --- \texttt{known exposure map}; \(f_i:\{0,1\}^n\to\Delta_i\); \texttt{causal design map}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The exposure mapping induced by \(G\) for unit \(i\).
  \item \texttt{Y\_\allowbreak{}i(w)} --- \texttt{assignment-\allowbreak{}indexed potential outcome}; \(Y_i:\{0,1\}^n\to[0,1]\); \texttt{primitive potential-\allowbreak{}outcome schedule}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The potential outcome of unit \(i\) under assignment \(w\).
  \item \texttt{D\_\allowbreak{}\{i,\allowbreak{}a\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{exposure indicator}; \(\{0,1\}\); \texttt{design random variable}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The indicator \(D_{i,a}=\mathbf 1\{f_i(W)=a\}\).
  \item \texttt{D} --- \texttt{stacked exposure-\allowbreak{}indicator vector}; \(\{0,1\}^{2n}\); \texttt{control-\allowbreak{}variate primitive}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The vector \(D=(D_{1,1},\ldots,D_{n,1},D_{1,0},\ldots,D_{n,0})^{\top}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}pi\_\allowbreak{}\{i,\allowbreak{}a\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{exposure probability}; \((0,1]\); \texttt{design functional}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The known probability \(\pi_{i,a}=\mathbb E_{\nu}[D_{i,a}]\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}Pi} --- \texttt{exposure-\allowbreak{}probability matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{2n\times 2n}\); \texttt{known design matrix}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The diagonal matrix whose diagonal is \((\pi_{1,1},\ldots,\pi_{n,1},\pi_{1,0},\ldots,\pi_{n,0})\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}Omega} --- \texttt{exposure covariance matrix}; \(\mathbb S_+^{2n}\); \texttt{known design matrix}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The known matrix \(\Omega=\operatorname{Var}_{\nu}(D)\).
  \item \texttt{Y\_\allowbreak{}i(a)} --- \texttt{potential outcome}; \([0,1]\); \texttt{causal primitive}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The potential outcome of unit \(i\) under exposure label \(a\).
  \item \texttt{H} --- \texttt{joint potential-\allowbreak{}outcome law}; \(\mathcal P([0,1]^2)\); \texttt{dependence-\allowbreak{}ambiguous state}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The superpopulation law of the pair \((Y_i(1),Y_i(0))\) used only for ex ante basis choice.
  \item \texttt{F\_\allowbreak{}a} --- \texttt{potential-\allowbreak{}outcome marginal law}; \(\mathcal P([0,1])\); \texttt{pilot-\allowbreak{}identified nuisance}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The marginal law of \(Y_i(a)\) under \(H\).
  \item \texttt{F\_\allowbreak{}1} --- \texttt{focal-\allowbreak{}exposure marginal law}; \(\mathcal P([0,1])\); \texttt{pilot-\allowbreak{}identified nuisance}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The marginal \(F_a\) at \(a=1\).
  \item \texttt{F\_\allowbreak{}0} --- \texttt{comparison-\allowbreak{}exposure marginal law}; \(\mathcal P([0,1])\); \texttt{pilot-\allowbreak{}identified nuisance}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The marginal \(F_a\) at \(a=0\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}Gamma(F\_\allowbreak{}1,\allowbreak{}F\_\allowbreak{}0)} --- \texttt{fixed-\allowbreak{}marginal coupling class}; \(2^{\mathcal P([0,1]^2)}\); \texttt{dependence-\allowbreak{}ambiguity class}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The class \(\Gamma(F_1,F_0)=\{H\in\mathcal P([0,1]^2):\operatorname{marg}(H)=(F_1,F_0)\}\).
  \item \texttt{F\_\allowbreak{}a\textasciicircum{}\{-\allowbreak{}1\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{left-\allowbreak{}continuous quantile function}; \(F_a^{-1}(u)=\inf\{y:F_a(y)\ge u\}\); \texttt{Fréchet endpoint functional}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The generalized inverse of the marginal distribution function.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mu\_\allowbreak{}a} --- \texttt{marginal mean}; \([0,1]\); \texttt{pilot-\allowbreak{}identified moment}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The mean \(\mu_a=\int y\,dF_a(y)\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}sigma\_\allowbreak{}a\textasciicircum{}2} --- \texttt{marginal variance}; \([0,1/4]\); \texttt{pilot-\allowbreak{}identified moment}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The variance \(\sigma_a^2=\int(y-\mu_a)^2\,dF_a(y)\).
  \item \texttt{c\_\allowbreak{}L} --- \texttt{lower Fréchet covariance endpoint}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{sharp dependence endpoint}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The value \(c_L=\int_0^1F_1^{-1}(u)F_0^{-1}(1-u)\,du-\mu_1\mu_0\).
  \item \texttt{c\_\allowbreak{}U} --- \texttt{upper Fréchet covariance endpoint}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{sharp dependence endpoint}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The value \(c_U=\int_0^1F_1^{-1}(u)F_0^{-1}(u)\,du-\mu_1\mu_0\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal C(F\_\allowbreak{}1,\allowbreak{}F\_\allowbreak{}0)} --- \texttt{feasible cross-\allowbreak{}world covariance domain}; \(2^{\mathbb R}\); \texttt{typed dependence domain}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The covariance image \(\mathcal C(F_1,F_0)=\{\operatorname{Cov}_H(Y_i(1),Y_i(0)):H\in\Gamma(F_1,F_0)\}\).
  \item \texttt{c} --- \texttt{feasible cross-\allowbreak{}world covariance}; \(\mathcal C(F_1,F_0)\); \texttt{unidentified dependence functional}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The scalar \(c=\operatorname{Cov}_H(Y_i(1),Y_i(0))\) for \(H\in\Gamma(F_1,F_0)\).
  \item \texttt{Q(c)} --- \texttt{feasible-\allowbreak{}covariance second-\allowbreak{}moment matrix}; \(\mathbb S_+^{2n}\); \texttt{ex ante outcome moment}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} For \(c\in\mathcal C(F_1,F_0)\), the matrix \(Q(c)=\begin{bmatrix}\mu_1^2\mathbf 1\mathbf 1^{\top}+\sigma_1^2I_n&-\mu_1\mu_0\mathbf 1\mathbf 1^{\top}-cI_n\\-\mu_1\mu_0\mathbf 1\mathbf 1^{\top}-cI_n&\mu_0^2\mathbf 1\mathbf 1^{\top}+\sigma_0^2I_n\end{bmatrix}\).
  \item \texttt{b\_\allowbreak{}1} --- \texttt{focal-\allowbreak{}exposure basis vector}; \(\mathbb R^n\); \texttt{basis decision}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The coefficients multiplying the focal-\allowbreak{}exposure indicators.}
  \item \texttt{b\_\allowbreak{}0} --- \texttt{comparison-\allowbreak{}exposure basis vector}; \(\mathbb R^n\); \texttt{basis decision}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The coefficients multiplying the comparison-\allowbreak{}exposure indicators.}
  \item \texttt{B} --- \texttt{block-\allowbreak{}separable basis matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{2n\times2}\); \texttt{projective decision variable}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The matrix \(B(b_1,b_0)=\begin{bmatrix}b_1&0\\0&b_0\end{bmatrix}\).
  \item \texttt{S\_\allowbreak{}B} --- \texttt{control-\allowbreak{}variate covariance scale}; \(\mathbb S_+^2\); \texttt{basis regularity matrix}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The matrix \(S_B=B^{\top}\Omega B\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}kappa} --- \texttt{regularity threshold}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{feasible-\allowbreak{}class index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} A fixed lower bound on the normalized smallest eigenvalue of \(S_B\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widetilde\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal B\}\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}Z} --- \texttt{unquotiented positive-\allowbreak{}definite rank-\allowbreak{}two restriction}; \(2^{\mathbb R^{2n\times2}}\); \texttt{rank-\allowbreak{}two ordinary-\allowbreak{}inverse restriction}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The block-\allowbreak{}separable matrices (B) satisfying (B\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}top\}\allowbreak{}Omega Bsucc0);\allowbreak{} this is only the rank-\allowbreak{}two restriction of Zhao's published unquotiented all-\allowbreak{}block-\allowbreak{}separable pseudoinverse domain.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal B\_\allowbreak{}Z} --- \texttt{positive-\allowbreak{}definite rank-\allowbreak{}two projective basis restriction}; \(2^{\mathbb {RP}^{n-1}\times\mathbb {RP}^{n-1}}\); \texttt{rank-\allowbreak{}two projective restriction}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The projective quotient of (\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widetilde\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal B\}\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}Z).}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal B\_\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}kappa} --- \texttt{normalized regular projective basis class}; \(2^{\mathbb {RP}^{n-1}\times\mathbb {RP}^{n-1}}\); \texttt{robust optimization domain}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The compact normalized subclass with \(\lambda_{\min}(n^{-1}S_B)\ge\kappa\).
  \item \texttt{g\_\allowbreak{}B(c)} --- \texttt{ex ante variance gain}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{basis objective}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} For \(B\in\mathcal B_Z\) and \(c\in\mathcal C(F_1,F_0)\), the Zhao gain \(g_B(c)=n^{-2}\operatorname{tr}\!\left[S_B^{-1}B^{\top}\Omega\Pi^{-1}Q(c)\Pi^{-1}\Omega B\right]\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}ell\_\allowbreak{}B(c)} --- \texttt{ex ante residual design variance}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{worst-\allowbreak{}copula performance}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} For \(B\in\mathcal B_Z\) and \(c\in\mathcal C(F_1,F_0)\), the residual \(\ell_B(c)=n^{-2}\operatorname{tr}[\Pi^{-1}\Omega\Pi^{-1}Q(c)]-g_B(c)\).
  \item \texttt{r(B)} --- \texttt{secondary robust guaranteed gain}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{secondary maximin-\allowbreak{}gain diagnostic}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The diagnostic \(r(B)=\min\{g_B(c_L),g_B(c_U)\}\).
  \item \texttt{w(B)} --- \texttt{worst-\allowbreak{}copula residual variance}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{secondary fixed-\allowbreak{}threshold rank-\allowbreak{}two minimax criterion}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The primary loss \(w(B)=\max\{\ell_B(c_L),\ell_B(c_U)\}\).
  \item \texttt{v\textasciicircum{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}star} --- \texttt{secondary optimal maximin gain}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{secondary maximin-\allowbreak{}gain value}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The diagnostic value \(v^\star=\max_{B\in\mathcal B_\kappa}r(B)\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal A\_\allowbreak{}R} --- \texttt{secondary maximin-\allowbreak{}gain argmax set}; \(2^{\mathcal B_\kappa}\); \texttt{secondary maximin-\allowbreak{}gain decision set}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The diagnostic correspondence \(\mathcal A_R=\operatorname*{argmax}_{B\in\mathcal B_\kappa}r(B)\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal A\_\allowbreak{}0} --- \texttt{zero-\allowbreak{}covariance surrogate argmax set}; \(2^{\mathcal B_\kappa}\); \texttt{secondary fixed-\allowbreak{}threshold rank-\allowbreak{}two zero-\allowbreak{}covariance set}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The correspondence \(\mathcal A_0=\operatorname*{argmax}_{B\in\mathcal B_\kappa}g_B(0)\) on the fixed normalized regular projective class; it is not Zhao's optimizer correspondence on the published unquotiented domain.
  \item \texttt{m\_\allowbreak{}a} --- \texttt{pilot sample size}; \(\mathbb N\); \texttt{pilot index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The number of independent unpaired pilot outcomes observed under exposure label \(a\).
  \item \texttt{Y\textasciicircum{}\{P\}\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\{j,\allowbreak{}a\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{unpaired pilot outcome}; \([0,1]\); \texttt{pilot observation}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The \(j\)-th independent pilot draw from \(F_a\), with no cross-arm pairing.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat F\_\allowbreak{}a} --- \texttt{empirical pilot marginal}; \(\mathcal P([0,1])\); \texttt{pilot estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The empirical law \(\widehat F_a=m_a^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{m_a}\delta_{Y^P_{j,a}}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat r(B)} --- \texttt{plug-\allowbreak{}in secondary guaranteed gain}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{secondary pilot objective}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The diagnostic obtained by substituting empirical marginal moments and endpoints into \(r(B)\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat v} --- \texttt{plug-\allowbreak{}in secondary maximin-\allowbreak{}gain value}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{secondary pilot value}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The diagnostic value \(\widehat v=\max_{B\in\mathcal B_\kappa}\widehat r(B)\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal A\}\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}R} --- \texttt{plug-\allowbreak{}in secondary maximin-\allowbreak{}gain argmax set}; \(2^{\mathcal B_\kappa}\); \texttt{secondary pilot decision set}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The diagnostic set \(\widehat{\mathcal A}_R=\operatorname*{argmax}_{B\in\mathcal B_\kappa}\widehat r(B)\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon} --- \texttt{global-\allowbreak{}certification tolerance}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{algorithmic accuracy}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The requested numerical optimality gap.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathfrak I\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{cert\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{moment-\allowbreak{}complete certified algorithmic input}; \(\mathsf{Enc}(\Pi,\Omega,Q(c_L),Q(c_U),c_L,c_U,\kappa,\varepsilon)\); \texttt{corrected algorithmic interface}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{An exact rational or real-\allowbreak{}algebraic encoding,\allowbreak{} or certified inclusion-\allowbreak{}oracle encoding with effective refinement moduli and exact feasibility decisions,\allowbreak{} of the known design matrices,\allowbreak{} both endpoint moment matrices,\allowbreak{} endpoint covariances,\allowbreak{} regularity threshold,\allowbreak{} and tolerance.}
  \item \texttt{h\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{certificate mesh diameter}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{outer-\allowbreak{}set refinement index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The maximum projective-chart diameter of a box retained at tolerance \(\varepsilon\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}xi} --- \texttt{lawful design-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}marginal instance}; \(\mathcal P(\{0,1\}^n)\times\mathcal P([0,1])^2\); \texttt{neighborhood parameter}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The instance \(\xi=(\nu,F_1,F_0)\) with \(G\) and the exposure maps held fixed.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathfrak X\_\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}kappa} --- \texttt{lawful instance class}; \(2^{\mathcal P(\{0,1\}^n)\times\mathcal P([0,1])^2}\); \texttt{perturbation domain}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The relatively topologized class of instances satisfying the named predicates ass:\allowbreak{}exposure-\allowbreak{}positivity and ass:\allowbreak{}kappa-\allowbreak{}feasibility.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}tau} --- \texttt{finite-\allowbreak{}population exposure effect}; \([-1,1]\); \texttt{target estimand}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The causal contrast \(\tau=n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^n\{Y_i(1)-Y_i(0)\}\).
  \item \texttt{h(c)} --- \texttt{uncontrolled ex ante variance}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{endpoint-\allowbreak{}dependent uncontrolled variance}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} For \(c\in\mathcal C(F_1,F_0)\), \(h(c)=n^{-2}\operatorname{tr}[\Pi^{-1}\Omega\Pi^{-1}Q(c)]\).
  \item \texttt{v\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{mm\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{optimal minimax residual value}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{secondary fixed-\allowbreak{}threshold rank-\allowbreak{}two robust value}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The value \(v_{\mathrm{mm}}=\min_{B\in\mathcal B_\kappa}w(B)\).
  \item \texttt{B\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{certified feasible minimax incumbent}; \(\mathcal B_\kappa\); \texttt{minimization certificate component}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} An exactly feasible basis used to certify an upper bound on \(v_{\mathrm{mm}}\).
  \item \texttt{L\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{certified lower minimax-\allowbreak{}value bound}; \(\mathbb Q\); \texttt{minimization certificate component}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} A rational lower bound on \(v_{\mathrm{mm}}\) obtained from outward-rounded lower bounds over all retained feasible boxes.
  \item \texttt{U\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{certified upper minimax-\allowbreak{}value bound}; \(\mathbb Q\); \texttt{minimization certificate component}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} A rational upper bound on \(v_{\mathrm{mm}}\) certified by an exactly feasible incumbent and outward rounding.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal A\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{mm\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{minimax residual basis argmin set}; \(2^{\mathcal B_\kappa}\); \texttt{secondary fixed-\allowbreak{}threshold rank-\allowbreak{}two minimax set}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The correspondence \(\mathcal A_{\mathrm{mm}}=\operatorname*{argmin}_{B\in\mathcal B_\kappa}w(B)\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal C\_\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon} --- \texttt{certified outer minimizer set}; \(2^{\mathcal B_\kappa}\); \texttt{global minimization certificate}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The union of unpruned quotient-chart boxes, required to contain \(\mathcal A_{\mathrm{mm}}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathscr K\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{finite minimax global-\allowbreak{}optimality certificate}; \texttt{candidate certified minimax output}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A checkable record of the chart cover,\allowbreak{} feasible incumbent,\allowbreak{} minimization bounds,\allowbreak{} retained outer boxes,\allowbreak{} exact infeasibility or lower-\allowbreak{}bound pruning records,\allowbreak{} and mesh.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}Delta(\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}xi)} --- \texttt{minimax-\allowbreak{}versus-\allowbreak{}surrogate separation}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{strict-\allowbreak{}dominance functional}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The gap \(\Delta(\xi)=\inf_{B_0\in\mathcal A_0(\xi)}w_\xi(B_0)-\sup_{B_{\mathrm{mm}}\in\mathcal A_{\mathrm{mm}}(\xi)}w_\xi(B_{\mathrm{mm}})\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}delta} --- \texttt{certified strict minimax-\allowbreak{}dominance margin}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{witness certificate component}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} A positive certified lower bound on \(\Delta(\xi)\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}eta} --- \texttt{certified optimizer regularity slack}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{witness certificate component}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} A uniform positive lower bound on normalized nondegeneracy above \(\kappa\) over \(\mathcal A_{\mathrm{mm}}\cup\mathcal A_0\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat w(B)} --- \texttt{plug-\allowbreak{}in worst-\allowbreak{}copula residual loss}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{secondary regular rank-\allowbreak{}two pilot objective}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The endpoint residual criterion obtained by substituting the empirical marginal moments and Frechet covariance endpoints into \(w(B)\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat v\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{mm\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{plug-\allowbreak{}in minimax residual value}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{secondary regular rank-\allowbreak{}two pilot value}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The value \(\widehat v_{\mathrm{mm}}=\min_{B\in\mathcal B_\kappa}\widehat w(B)\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal A\}\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{mm\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{plug-\allowbreak{}in minimax residual argmin set}; \(2^{\mathcal B_\kappa}\); \texttt{secondary regular rank-\allowbreak{}two pilot correspondence}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The set \(\widehat{\mathcal A}_{\mathrm{mm}}=\operatorname*{argmin}_{B\in\mathcal B_\kappa}\widehat w(B)\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal B\_\allowbreak{}Z\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{projective quotient of Zhao's published all-\allowbreak{}block-\allowbreak{}separable pseudoinverse domain}; \(2^{(\mathbb R^n\cup\{0\})\times(\mathbb R^n\cup\{0\})}\); \texttt{primary full pseudoinverse decision domain}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The note's exact scale-\allowbreak{}invariant projective quotient of Zhao's published unquotiented all-\allowbreak{}block-\allowbreak{}separable positive-\allowbreak{}semidefinite pseudoinverse carrier,\allowbreak{} including ranks zero,\allowbreak{} one,\allowbreak{} and two.}
  \item \texttt{S\_\allowbreak{}B\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{Zhao spectral pseudoinverse}; \(\mathbb S_+^2\); \texttt{published pseudoinverse convention}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{Zhao's positive-\allowbreak{}semidefinite spectral inverse of (S\_\allowbreak{}B=\allowbreak{}B\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}top\}\allowbreak{}Omega B),\allowbreak{} taking reciprocal positive eigenvalues and assigning zero to zero eigenvalues.}
  \item \texttt{g\_\allowbreak{}B\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}(c)} --- \texttt{full-\allowbreak{}carrier ex ante variance gain}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{primary full-\allowbreak{}carrier gain}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The Zhao-\allowbreak{}form pseudoinverse gain evaluated on the note's quotient (\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal B\_\allowbreak{}Z\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}).}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}ell\_\allowbreak{}B\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}(c)} --- \texttt{full-\allowbreak{}carrier residual variance}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{primary full-\allowbreak{}carrier loss}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The residual (h(c)-\allowbreak{}g\_\allowbreak{}B\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}(c)) on the note's quotient of Zhao's published carrier.}
  \item \texttt{w\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}(B)} --- \texttt{full-\allowbreak{}carrier worst-\allowbreak{}copula residual variance}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{primary robust criterion}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The maximum of (\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}ell\_\allowbreak{}B\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}(c\_\allowbreak{}L)) and (\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}ell\_\allowbreak{}B\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}(c\_\allowbreak{}U)) on (\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal B\_\allowbreak{}Z\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}).}
  \item \texttt{v\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{mm\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{full-\allowbreak{}carrier optimal minimax infimum}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{primary robust value}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The infimum of (w\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}(B)) over the note's projective quotient (\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal B\_\allowbreak{}Z\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{});\allowbreak{} it need not be attained.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal A\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{mm\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{full-\allowbreak{}carrier minimax equality set}; \(2^{\mathcal B_Z^+}\); \texttt{primary full-\allowbreak{}carrier equality correspondence}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The possibly empty set of classes in (\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal B\_\allowbreak{}Z\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}) satisfying (w\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}(B)=\allowbreak{}v\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{mm\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}).}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal A\_\allowbreak{}0\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{full-\allowbreak{}carrier zero-\allowbreak{}covariance equality set}; \(2^{\mathcal B_Z^+}\); \texttt{full-\allowbreak{}carrier surrogate correspondence}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The possibly empty argmax of (g\_\allowbreak{}B\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}(0)) over the note's quotient (\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal B\_\allowbreak{}Z\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}).}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}iota} --- \texttt{rank-\allowbreak{}two inclusion into the full quotient}; \(\iota:\mathcal B_Z\to\mathcal B_Z^+\); \texttt{rank-\allowbreak{}stratum comparison map}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The bijection from the ordinary rank-\allowbreak{}two projective class (\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal B\_\allowbreak{}Z) onto the rank-\allowbreak{}two stratum of the note's quotient (\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal B\_\allowbreak{}Z\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}).}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal A\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{mm\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}(\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}kappa)} --- \texttt{threshold-\allowbreak{}indexed minimax argmin set}; \(2^{\mathcal B_\kappa}\); \texttt{value-\allowbreak{}transfer correspondence}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The set \(\operatorname*{argmin}_{B\in\mathcal B_\kappa}w(B)\) with the threshold shown explicitly.
  \item \texttt{Y\_\allowbreak{}i\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{obs\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{observed outcome}; \([0,1]\); \texttt{observable estimator input}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The realized outcome \(Y_i(W)\) in the main experiment.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat Y\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{obs\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{signed armwise Horvitz-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}Thompson outcome stack}; \(\mathbb R^{2n}\); \texttt{observable coefficient input}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The vector with entries \(D_{i,1}Y_i^{\mathrm{obs}}/\pi_{i,1}\) in the first block and \(-D_{i,0}Y_i^{\mathrm{obs}}/\pi_{i,0}\) in the second block.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}gamma\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{HT\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}(B)} --- \texttt{observable Zhao pseudoinverse control coefficient}; \(\mathbb R^2\); \texttt{observable coefficient map}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The coefficient (S\_\allowbreak{}B\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}B\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}top\}\allowbreak{}OmegaPi\textasciicircum{}\{-\allowbreak{}1\}\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat Y\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{obs\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}),\allowbreak{} equal to the ordinary-\allowbreak{}inverse formula on the regular rank-\allowbreak{}two class.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat 1\_\allowbreak{}a} --- \texttt{armwise inverse-\allowbreak{}probability normalizer}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{self-\allowbreak{}normalization primitive}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The average \(n^{-1}\sum_iD_{i,a}/\pi_{i,a}\), when its denominator is positive.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}gamma\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{SN\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}(B)} --- \texttt{self-\allowbreak{}normalized observable pseudoinverse control coefficient}; \(\mathbb R^2\); \texttt{unbridged implementation map}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The armwise self-\allowbreak{}normalized version of Zhao's pseudoinverse Horvitz-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}Thompson coefficient.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}tau\_\allowbreak{}B\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{CV\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{observable control-\allowbreak{}variate estimator}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{causal estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The Horvitz-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}Thompson exposure-\allowbreak{}effect estimator minus the chosen coefficient times the centered basis exposure statistic.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal J\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{PICC\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{reproducible unexecuted heuristic Jiangxi PICC reanalysis protocol}; \texttt{exploratory heuristic application protocol}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The checked local-\allowbreak{}Adam,\allowbreak{} positive-\allowbreak{}definite rank-\allowbreak{}two,\allowbreak{} ordinary-\allowbreak{}solve and self-\allowbreak{}normalized protocol;\allowbreak{} no numerical table or global certificate is asserted.}
  \item \texttt{Y} --- \texttt{signed finite-\allowbreak{}population exposure-\allowbreak{}outcome vector}; \(\mathbb R^{2n}\); \texttt{coefficient expectation target}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The vector \(Y=(Y_1(1),\ldots,Y_n(1),-Y_1(0),\ldots,-Y_n(0))^{\top}\).
  \item \texttt{q\_\allowbreak{}Z} --- \texttt{ordinary-\allowbreak{}domain quotient map}; \(q_Z:\widetilde{\mathcal B}_Z\to\mathcal B_Z\); \texttt{exact domain-\allowbreak{}transfer map}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The map \(q_Z(B)=[B]\) that quotients independent nonzero rescaling of the two arm columns.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}overline a} --- \texttt{coordinate-\allowbreak{}envelope constant}; \([1,\infty)\); \texttt{rate-\allowbreak{}class index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A fixed uniform bound on every coordinate of a normalized supplied basis.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal B\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}kappa,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}overline a\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{normalized Zhao-\allowbreak{}rate-\allowbreak{}admissible basis class}; \(2^{\mathcal B_\kappa}\); \texttt{uniform feasible-\allowbreak{}estimator domain}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The intersection of \(\mathcal B_\kappa\) with the coordinate envelope indexed by \(\overline a\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}alpha} --- \texttt{dependency-\allowbreak{}neighborhood exponent}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{design-\allowbreak{}rate exponent}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The exponent governing the maximum dependency-\allowbreak{}neighborhood size.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}beta} --- \texttt{inverse-\allowbreak{}exposure-\allowbreak{}probability exponent}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{design-\allowbreak{}rate exponent}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The exponent governing decay of the minimum exposure probability.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}underline\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}pi} --- \texttt{positive exposure-\allowbreak{}rate constant}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{design-\allowbreak{}rate constant}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The constant in the lower bound \(\pi_{i,a}\ge\underline\pi n^{-\beta}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat Q\_\allowbreak{}e} --- \texttt{pilot endpoint moment matrix}; \(\mathbb S_+^{2n}\); \texttt{pilot matrix estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The plug-\allowbreak{}in endpoint matrix obtained from the two empirical pilot marginals.}
  \item \texttt{C\_\allowbreak{}n} --- \texttt{endpoint-\allowbreak{}matrix sensitivity}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{uniform plug-\allowbreak{}in modulus}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The nuclear-\allowbreak{}norm sensitivity coefficient in lem:\allowbreak{}uniform-\allowbreak{}pilot-\allowbreak{}endpoint-\allowbreak{}matrix-\allowbreak{}rate.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat g\_\allowbreak{}B(c)} --- \texttt{plug-\allowbreak{}in endpoint gain}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{pilot objective}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The gain obtained by replacing the endpoint moment matrix by its pilot plug-\allowbreak{}in counterpart.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat B\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{mm\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{pilot-\allowbreak{}selected robust basis}; \(\mathcal B_{\kappa,\overline a}\); \texttt{pilot decision}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A measurable minimizer of the plug-\allowbreak{}in worst-\allowbreak{}endpoint loss.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat B\_\allowbreak{}0} --- \texttt{pilot-\allowbreak{}selected zero-\allowbreak{}covariance basis}; \(\mathcal B_{\kappa,\overline a}\); \texttt{pilot comparator decision}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A measurable maximizer of the plug-\allowbreak{}in zero-\allowbreak{}covariance gain.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}tau\_\allowbreak{}B\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{CV,\allowbreak{}SN\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{self-\allowbreak{}normalized implementation variant}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{unbridged implementation variant}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The control-variate estimator formed with \(\widehat\gamma^{\mathrm{SN}}(B)\), kept separate from the primary Horvitz--Thompson-coefficient estimator.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}gamma\_\allowbreak{}B\textasciicircum{}*(Y)} --- \texttt{fixed-\allowbreak{}array oracle control coefficient}; \(\mathbb R^2\); \texttt{oracle comparison}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The coefficient \((B^{\top}\Omega B)^{-1}B^{\top}\Omega\Pi^{-1}Y\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}tau\_\allowbreak{}B\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{or\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{fixed-\allowbreak{}array oracle control-\allowbreak{}variate estimator}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{variance-\allowbreak{}equivalence oracle}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The observable-map estimator with \(\gamma_B^*(Y)\) substituted for the estimated coefficient.
  \item \texttt{c\_\allowbreak{}H} --- \texttt{cross-\allowbreak{}world covariance under a coupling}; \(\mathcal C(F_1,F_0)\); \texttt{oracle ex ante state}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The covariance \(\operatorname{Cov}_H\{Y_i(1),Y_i(0)\}\).
  \item \texttt{P} --- \texttt{unpaired-\allowbreak{}pilot sampling law}; \texttt{pilot expectation law}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The joint product law of the two independent arm-\allowbreak{}specific pilot samples.}
  \item \texttt{v\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{mm\}\allowbreak{},\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}overline a\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{rate-\allowbreak{}class minimax residual value}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{pilot oracle value}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The minimum of \(w(B)\) over \(\mathcal B_{\kappa,\overline a}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal A\_\allowbreak{}\{0,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}overline a\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{rate-\allowbreak{}class zero-\allowbreak{}covariance argmax set}; \(2^{\mathcal B_{\kappa,\overline a}}\); \texttt{pilot comparator oracle set}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The argmax of \(g_B(0)\) over the rate-admissible class.
  \item \texttt{E} --- \texttt{cross-\allowbreak{}block exchange matrix}; \(\mathbb R^{2n\times2n}\); \texttt{affine covariance direction}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The matrix \(\begin{bmatrix}0&I_n\\I_n&0\end{bmatrix}\) satisfying \(Q(c)=Q(0)-cE\).
  \item \texttt{A\_\allowbreak{}B} --- \texttt{ordinary-\allowbreak{}domain gain kernel}; \(\mathbb S_+^{2n}\); \texttt{intercept-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}slope kernel}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The matrix \(\Pi^{-1}\Omega B(B^{\top}\Omega B)^{-1}B^{\top}\Omega\Pi^{-1}\).
  \item \texttt{a\_\allowbreak{}B} --- \texttt{ordinary-\allowbreak{}domain gain intercept}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{nominal gain coordinate}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The zero-covariance gain \(g_B(0)\).
  \item \texttt{b\_\allowbreak{}B} --- \texttt{ordinary-\allowbreak{}domain gain slope}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{copula-\allowbreak{}sensitivity coordinate}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The coefficient of \(c\) in \(g_B(c)=a_B+b_Bc\).
  \item \texttt{p\_\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}xi(b)} --- \texttt{endpoint drift penalty}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{robust slope penalty}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The maximum of \((2/n-b)c_L\) and \((2/n-b)c_U\).
  \item \texttt{a\textasciicircum{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}star} --- \texttt{optimal ordinary-\allowbreak{}domain zero-\allowbreak{}covariance gain}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{nominal optimum}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The attained maximum of \(a_B\) over \(\mathcal B_Z\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal A\_\allowbreak{}\{0,\allowbreak{}Z\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{ordinary-\allowbreak{}domain zero-\allowbreak{}covariance optimizer set}; \(2^{\mathcal B_Z}\); \texttt{ordinary-\allowbreak{}domain nominal correspondence}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The argmax of \(a_B\) over \(\mathcal B_Z\), when attained.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal A\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{mm\}\allowbreak{},\allowbreak{}Z\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{ordinary-\allowbreak{}domain minimax residual optimizer set}; \(2^{\mathcal B_Z}\); \texttt{ordinary-\allowbreak{}domain robust correspondence}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The argmin of \(w(B)\) over \(\mathcal B_Z\), when attained.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}Delta\_\allowbreak{}Z} --- \texttt{ordinary-\allowbreak{}domain robust improvement gap}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{strict-\allowbreak{}improvement certificate}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The worst-\allowbreak{}loss difference between the best zero-\allowbreak{}covariance optimizer and the ordinary-\allowbreak{}domain minimax value,\allowbreak{} when the extrema are attained.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon\_\allowbreak{}m} --- \texttt{pilot optimization tolerance}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{pilot approximation index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A deterministic or pilot-\allowbreak{}measurable positive tolerance for full-\allowbreak{}carrier empirical minimization.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat w\_\allowbreak{}m\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}(B)} --- \texttt{full-\allowbreak{}carrier empirical worst-\allowbreak{}endpoint loss}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{primary full-\allowbreak{}carrier pilot objective}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The projector/\allowbreak{}pseudoinverse worst-\allowbreak{}endpoint loss obtained by substituting the two unpaired-\allowbreak{}pilot endpoint matrices,\allowbreak{} kept distinct from the regular rank-\allowbreak{}two objective (\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat w(B)).}
  \item \texttt{r\_\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}Omega} --- \texttt{maximal achievable control rank}; \(\{0,1,2\}\); \texttt{full-\allowbreak{}carrier selector rank}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The integer \(r_\Omega=\max_{[B]_+\in\mathcal B_Z^+}\operatorname{rank}(B^{\top}\Omega B)\).
  \item \texttt{C\_\allowbreak{}k} --- \texttt{fixed normalized maximal-\allowbreak{}rank rational-\allowbreak{}support block class}; \(\mathcal B_Z^+\); \texttt{countable measurable-\allowbreak{}selector support}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The \(k\)-th class in the fixed enumeration of normalized rational-support block classes satisfying \(\operatorname{rank}(C_k^{\top}\Omega C_k)=r_\Omega\); when \(r_\Omega=0\), every term is the zero class.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat B\_\allowbreak{}\{m,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon\_\allowbreak{}m\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{classical nonconstructive measurable full-\allowbreak{}carrier positive-\allowbreak{}tolerance selector}; \(\mathcal B_Z^+\); \texttt{nonconstructive full-\allowbreak{}carrier pilot existence rule}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The first countable-enumeration class whose empirical loss is within \(\varepsilon_m\) of the countable infimum; it is a set-theoretic measurable existence selector, not a finite evaluation algorithm.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathfrak I\}\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\{m,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{alg\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{exact algebraically encoded empirical endpoint input}; \texttt{finite algorithmic pilot input}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The rational or real-\allowbreak{}algebraic empirical moment-\allowbreak{}complete input for the full-\allowbreak{}carrier rank-\allowbreak{}stratified CAD selector.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathscr K\}\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\{m,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon\_\allowbreak{}m\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{CAD\}\allowbreak{},\allowbreak{}+\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{finite empirical full-\allowbreak{}carrier CAD certificate}; \texttt{checkable pilot optimization certificate}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The selected-\allowbreak{}rank,\allowbreak{} algebraic-\allowbreak{}basis,\allowbreak{} value-\allowbreak{}isolation,\allowbreak{} spectral-\allowbreak{}isolation,\allowbreak{} and CAD record returned on exact empirical input.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat B\_\allowbreak{}\{m,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon\_\allowbreak{}m\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{CAD\}\allowbreak{},\allowbreak{}+\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{encoded CAD empirical positive-\allowbreak{}tolerance basis}; \(\mathcal B_Z^+\); \texttt{finite full-\allowbreak{}carrier pilot decision}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The normalized rational or real-\allowbreak{}algebraic basis component of the exact-\allowbreak{}input rank-\allowbreak{}stratified CAD certificate.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat v\_\allowbreak{}m\textasciicircum{}+\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{empirical full-\allowbreak{}carrier infimum value}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{CAD value target}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The infimum of \(\widehat w_m^+(B)\) over \(\mathcal B_Z^+\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat r\_\allowbreak{}\{m,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon\_\allowbreak{}m\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{CAD\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{exact selected control rank}; \(\{0,1,2\}\); \texttt{rank-\allowbreak{}adaptive deployment field}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The exact rank of the selected CAD basis covariance scale.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat L\_\allowbreak{}\{m,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon\_\allowbreak{}m\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{CAD\}\allowbreak{},\allowbreak{}+\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{certified empirical full-\allowbreak{}carrier lower value bound}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{positive-\allowbreak{}tolerance certificate field}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The exact algebraic lower bound on \(\widehat v_m^+\) in the CAD record.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat U\_\allowbreak{}\{m,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon\_\allowbreak{}m\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{CAD\}\allowbreak{},\allowbreak{}+\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{certified empirical full-\allowbreak{}carrier upper value bound}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{positive-\allowbreak{}tolerance certificate field}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The exact algebraic upper bound on \(\widehat v_m^+\) in the CAD record.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathscr S\}\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\{m,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon\_\allowbreak{}m\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{CAD\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{exact spectral-\allowbreak{}isolation record}; \texttt{rank-\allowbreak{}stability certificate field}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The characteristic polynomial,\allowbreak{} exact zero tags,\allowbreak{} multiplicities,\allowbreak{} and rational isolating intervals for the selected positive eigenvalues.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathscr P\}\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\{m,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon\_\allowbreak{}m\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{CAD\}\allowbreak{},\allowbreak{}+\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{rank-\allowbreak{}stratified CAD sign and value record}; \texttt{local checker input}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The finite cell,\allowbreak{} sign,\allowbreak{} sample,\allowbreak{} and algebraic value-\allowbreak{}isolation portion of the empirical CAD certificate.}
  \item \texttt{Z} --- \texttt{centered exposure vector}; \(\mathbb R^{2n}\); \texttt{design-\allowbreak{}moment primitive}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The vector \(Z=D-\mathbb E_\nu D\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}lambda\_\allowbreak{}+\allowbreak{}(S\_\allowbreak{}B)} --- \texttt{smallest positive control-\allowbreak{}scale eigenvalue}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{singular-\allowbreak{}rank stability margin}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} For positive control rank, the smallest strictly positive eigenvalue of \(S_B\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathfrak M\_\allowbreak{}\{3,\allowbreak{}n\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{exact third design-\allowbreak{}moment modulus}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{finite covariance-\allowbreak{}bridge modulus}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The maximum absolute third mixed moment of three sign-\allowbreak{}weighted centered exposure sums.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathfrak M\_\allowbreak{}\{4,\allowbreak{}n\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{exact fourth design-\allowbreak{}moment modulus}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{finite coefficient-\allowbreak{}error modulus}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The maximum fourth moment of a sign-\allowbreak{}weighted centered exposure sum.}
  \item \texttt{s\_\allowbreak{}n} --- \texttt{exact covariance-\allowbreak{}column support}; \(\{0,1,\ldots,2n\}\); \texttt{finite coefficient-\allowbreak{}sensitivity modulus}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The largest number of nonzero entries in a column of \(\Omega\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal S\_\allowbreak{}\{r,\allowbreak{}n\}\allowbreak{}(\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}lambda,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}overline a)} --- \texttt{rank-\allowbreak{}adaptive stability stratum}; \(2^{\mathcal B_Z^+}\); \texttt{singular-\allowbreak{}rank inference domain}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The normalized rank-\(r\) classes with coordinate envelope \(\overline a\) and, at positive rank, smallest positive eigenvalue at least \(n\lambda\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}gamma\_\allowbreak{}B\textasciicircum{}\{*,\allowbreak{}+\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}(Y)} --- \texttt{pseudoinverse fixed-\allowbreak{}array oracle coefficient}; \(\mathbb R^2\); \texttt{all-\allowbreak{}rank oracle comparison}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The coefficient \(S_B^+B^{\top}\Omega\Pi^{-1}Y\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}tau\_\allowbreak{}B\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{or\}\allowbreak{},\allowbreak{}+\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{pseudoinverse oracle control-\allowbreak{}variate estimator}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{all-\allowbreak{}rank oracle comparison}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The observable control-variate map with \(\gamma_B^{*,+}(Y)\) substituted for the estimated coefficient.
  \item \texttt{R\_\allowbreak{}\{n,\allowbreak{}r\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{ad\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{rank-\allowbreak{}adaptive finite variance-\allowbreak{}bridge envelope}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{selected-\allowbreak{}rank variance error}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The explicit third/fourth-moment bound for rank \(r\in\{1,2\}\) on a positive-eigenvalue stability stratum.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathscr G\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{CAD\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{primitive CAD/\allowbreak{}KKT geometry certificate}; \texttt{geometry optimality certificate}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The finite exact inside/\allowbreak{}outside gap,\allowbreak{} KKT,\allowbreak{} strict-\allowbreak{}complementarity,\allowbreak{} Hessian,\allowbreak{} remainder,\allowbreak{} and away-\allowbreak{}set record used to derive containment and a global nominal-\allowbreak{}to-\allowbreak{}robust error constant.}
  \item \texttt{d\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{pr\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{semialgebraic projective-\allowbreak{}chart distance}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{local optimizer geometry}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The minimum quotient-\allowbreak{}chart Euclidean distance after the fixed signed-\allowbreak{}pivot representative convention.}
  \item \texttt{N\_\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}ell} --- \texttt{directed local dependence neighborhood}; \(2^{\{1,\ldots,2n\}}\); \texttt{assignment-\allowbreak{}dependence primitive}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The supplied neighborhood of exposure coordinate \(\ell\), containing \(\ell\), in Zhao's coordinate-versus-complement local independence condition.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}overline d} --- \texttt{directed-\allowbreak{}neighborhood-\allowbreak{}size constant}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{design-\allowbreak{}rate constant}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The constant in the bound \(\max_{1\le \ell\le 2n}|N_\ell|\le\overline d n^{\alpha}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal G\_\allowbreak{}n} --- \texttt{directed neighborhood relation}; \(2^{\{1,\ldots,2n\}^2}\); \texttt{directed assignment-\allowbreak{}dependence notation}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The directed relation \(\mathcal G_n=\{(\ell,j):j\in N_\ell\}\), retained only as notation for the local neighborhoods and never used as a dependency graph.
  \item \texttt{d\_\allowbreak{}n} --- \texttt{exact directed-\allowbreak{}neighborhood size}; \(\{1,\ldots,2n\}\); \texttt{design-\allowbreak{}dependence modulus}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The quantity \(d_n=\max_{1\le\ell\le2n}|N_\ell|\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}lambda\_\allowbreak{}\{1,\allowbreak{}n\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{rank-\allowbreak{}one stability threshold}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{stable-\allowbreak{}carrier index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The displayed lower bound in \(\lambda_+(S_B)\ge n\lambda_{1,n}\) on the stable rank-one stratum.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}lambda\_\allowbreak{}\{2,\allowbreak{}n\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{rank-\allowbreak{}two stability threshold}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{stable-\allowbreak{}carrier index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The displayed lower bound in \(\lambda_+(S_B)\ge n\lambda_{2,n}\) on the stable rank-two stratum.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal K\_\allowbreak{}n\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{st\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{compact stable all-\allowbreak{}rank carrier}; \(2^{\mathcal B_Z^+}\); \texttt{stable deployment decision class}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The union of the rank-\allowbreak{}zero fallback and the rank-\allowbreak{}one and rank-\allowbreak{}two strata with displayed spectral thresholds and coordinate envelope.}
  \item \texttt{v\_\allowbreak{}n\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{st\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{stable-\allowbreak{}carrier optimal residual value}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{stable oracle value}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The attained minimum of \(w^+(B)\) over \(\mathcal K_n^{\mathrm{st}}\).
  \item \texttt{b\_\allowbreak{}n\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{st\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{exact stability price}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{approximation-\allowbreak{}versus-\allowbreak{}stability elbow}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The difference \(b_n^{\mathrm{st}}=v_n^{\mathrm{st}}-v_{\mathrm{mm}}^+\).
  \item \texttt{C\_\allowbreak{}k\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{st\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{fixed dense stable-\allowbreak{}carrier sequence}; \(\mathcal K_n^{\mathrm{st}}\); \texttt{nonconstructive stable-\allowbreak{}selector support}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The \(k\)-th term of a fixed countable dense sequence in the compact stable carrier.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat v\_\allowbreak{}m\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{st\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{empirical stable-\allowbreak{}carrier minimum}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{stable pilot value}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The minimum of \(\widehat w_m^+(B)\) over \(\mathcal K_n^{\mathrm{st}}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat k\_\allowbreak{}m\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{st\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{stable first-\allowbreak{}qualifying index}; \(\mathbb N\); \texttt{nonconstructive measurable selection index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The first dense-sequence index whose empirical stable loss is within \(\varepsilon_m\) of \(\widehat v_m^{\mathrm{st}}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat B\_\allowbreak{}\{m,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon\_\allowbreak{}m\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{st\}\allowbreak{},\allowbreak{}+\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{classical nonconstructive stable positive-\allowbreak{}tolerance selector}; \(\mathcal K_n^{\mathrm{st}}\); \texttt{stable arbitrary-\allowbreak{}real pilot decision}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The class \(C_{\widehat k_m^{\mathrm{st}}}^{\mathrm{st}}\) selected from the fixed dense stable sequence.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathfrak I\}\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\{m,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{alg\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{st\}\allowbreak{},\allowbreak{}+\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{augmented algebraic stable-\allowbreak{}selector input}; \texttt{finite stable CAD input}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The exact empirical endpoint input augmented by exact stable thresholds,\allowbreak{} coordinate envelope,\allowbreak{} and exact population endpoint input for stability-\allowbreak{}price certification.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathscr K\}\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\{m,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon\_\allowbreak{}m\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{CAD,\allowbreak{}st\}\allowbreak{},\allowbreak{}+\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{finite stable empirical CAD certificate}; \texttt{checkable stable optimization certificate}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The empirical stable-\allowbreak{}value,\allowbreak{} basis,\allowbreak{} rank,\allowbreak{} spectral,\allowbreak{} coordinate,\allowbreak{} and population stability-\allowbreak{}price record.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat B\_\allowbreak{}\{m,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon\_\allowbreak{}m\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{CAD,\allowbreak{}st\}\allowbreak{},\allowbreak{}+\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{encoded stable CAD pilot basis}; \(\mathcal K_n^{\mathrm{st}}\); \texttt{finite stable pilot decision}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The algebraic stable-basis component of \(\widehat{\mathscr K}_{m,\varepsilon_m}^{\mathrm{CAD,st},+}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}underline b\_\allowbreak{}\{n,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon\_\allowbreak{}m\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{st\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{certified lower stability-\allowbreak{}price bound}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{stability-\allowbreak{}price certificate field}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The lower endpoint of the CAD enclosure of \(b_n^{\mathrm{st}}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}overline b\_\allowbreak{}\{n,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon\_\allowbreak{}m\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{st\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{certified upper stability-\allowbreak{}price bound}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{stability-\allowbreak{}price certificate field}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The upper endpoint of the CAD enclosure of \(b_n^{\mathrm{st}}\).
  \item \texttt{V\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{st\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{stable-\allowbreak{}selector observable worst-\allowbreak{}copula performance}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{end-\allowbreak{}to-\allowbreak{}end observable performance}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The pilot expectation of the worst-\allowbreak{}coupling ex ante expected conditional design variance of the observable estimator deployed with the stable selected basis.}
\end{itemize}

\section{Assumptions}

\begin{assumption}[ass:known-exposure]\label{ass:known-exposure}
For every unit \(i\) and assignments \(w,w'\in\{0,1\}^n\), \(f_i(w)=f_i(w')\) implies \(Y_i(w)=Y_i(w')\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Known exposure mapping, \cite{AronowSamii2017}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:mutually-exclusive-exposures]\label{ass:mutually-exclusive-exposures}
For every unit \(i\), \(D_{i,1}D_{i,0}=0\) holds \(\nu\)-almost surely.
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Mutually exclusive exposure events, \cite{Zhao2026OptimalControlVariates}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:exposure-positivity]\label{ass:exposure-positivity}
For every unit \(i\) and label \(a\in\mathbb A\), \(\pi_{i,a}>0\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Exposure positivity, \cite{AronowSamii2017}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:iid-potential-pairs]\label{ass:iid-potential-pairs}
The \(n\) pairs \((Y_i(1),Y_i(0))\) have joint law \(H^{\otimes n}\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Independent and identically distributed potential-outcome pairs, Zhao Assumption 3\(^{\dagger}\) on p. 22, \cite{Zhao2026OptimalControlVariates}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:bounded-outcomes]\label{ass:bounded-outcomes}
The law \(H\) assigns probability one to \([0,1]^2\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Bounded potential outcomes, \cite{AronowGreenLee2014SharpVariance}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:fixed-marginals]\label{ass:fixed-marginals}
The ordered pair of marginals of \(H\) equals \((F_1,F_0)\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Fixed-marginal coupling restriction, \cite{Bergin1999RestrictedMarginals}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:frechet-covariance-domain]\label{ass:frechet-covariance-domain}
The feasible covariance image satisfies \(\mathcal C(F_1,F_0)=[c_L,c_U]\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Sharp Fréchet covariance range, Aronow--Green--Lee Lemma 1, \cite{AronowGreenLee2014SharpVariance}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:product-coupling-zero]\label{ass:product-coupling-zero}
The pair \(\left(F_1\otimes F_0,\operatorname{Cov}_{F_1\otimes F_0}\{Y_i(1),Y_i(0)\}\right)\) belongs to \(\Gamma(F_1,F_0)\times\{0\}\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Independent product coupling with zero covariance, \cite{AronowGreenLee2014SharpVariance}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:block-separable-basis]\label{ass:block-separable-basis}
The basis satisfies \(B=\begin{bmatrix}b_1&0\\0&b_0\end{bmatrix}\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Block-separable exposure-indicator basis, \cite{Zhao2026OptimalControlVariates}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:basis-invertibility]\label{ass:basis-invertibility}
The matrix \(S_B\) satisfies \(\lambda_{\min}(S_B)>0\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Supplied-basis control-variate nondegeneracy, Zhao Assumption 2\(^{\dagger}\) on p. 21, \cite{Zhao2026OptimalControlVariates}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:projective-normalization]\label{ass:projective-normalization}
The ordered norm pair satisfies \((\lVert b_1\rVert_2^2,\lVert b_0\rVert_2^2)=(n,n)\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Novel.} Independent rescaling of either basis column leaves Zhao's control-variate span and gain unchanged, so this condition selects compact representatives without restricting implementable estimators.
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:kappa-regularity]\label{ass:kappa-regularity}
The normalized covariance scale satisfies \(\lambda_{\min}(n^{-1}S_B)\ge\kappa\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Supplied-basis nondegeneracy, fixed-threshold variant of Zhao Assumption 2\(^{\dagger}\) on p. 21, \cite{Zhao2026OptimalControlVariates}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:kappa-feasibility]\label{ass:kappa-feasibility}
There exist vectors \(b_1,b_0\in\mathbb R^n\) satisfying \(\lVert b_1\rVert_2^2=\lVert b_0\rVert_2^2=n\) and \(\lambda_{\min}\{n^{-1}B^{\top}\Omega B\}\ge\kappa\) for \(B=\begin{bmatrix}b_1&0\\0&b_0\end{bmatrix}\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Novel.} This is a verifiable design-side condition: for a fixed network, exposure maps, and known randomization, the matrix \(\Omega\) is known before outcomes are observed, so nonemptiness of the normalized \(\kappa\)-regular projective class can be certified as part of design preparation. Zhao's Assumption 2\(^{\dagger}\) regulates a supplied basis and does not assert existence of such a normalized feasible basis.
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:unpaired-pilot]\label{ass:unpaired-pilot}
The two arm-specific pilot samples are mutually unpaired i.i.d. draws from \(F_1\) and \(F_0\), respectively, and their joint law is independent of \(W\sim\nu\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Novel.} Separate exposure-specific historical samples are available in insurance and public-health deployments even when privacy or record linkage prevents cross-arm pairing; independence from the later randomized assignment is ensured by collecting or freezing those archives before randomization. Mikhaeil and Green inform consistency of empirical marginal endpoints, not this product sampling law.
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:zhao-coordinate-envelope]\label{ass:zhao-coordinate-envelope}
For every supplied normalized basis, \(\max_{1\le i\le n,\,a\in\{1,0\}}|b_{a,i}|\le\overline a\), where \(\overline a\ge1\) is fixed along the triangular sequence.
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Zhao2026OptimalControlVariates, Assumption 2\(^{\dagger}\), supplied-basis bound, \cite{Zhao2026OptimalControlVariates}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:zhao-exposure-probability-rate]\label{ass:zhao-exposure-probability-rate}
There are constants \(\underline\pi>0\) and \(\beta\ge0\), independent of \(n\), such that \(\min_{1\le i\le n,\,a\in\{1,0\}}\pi_{i,a}\ge\underline\pi n^{-\beta}\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Zhao2026OptimalControlVariates, network exposure-probability rate condition, \cite{Zhao2026OptimalControlVariates}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:zhao-dependency-neighborhood-rate]\label{ass:zhao-dependency-neighborhood-rate}
For \(N=2n\) and every \(\ell\in\{1,\ldots,N\}\), the directed neighborhood satisfies \(\ell\in N_\ell\subseteq\{1,\ldots,N\}\), the variable \(D_\ell\) is independent under \(\nu\) of the entire subvector \((D_j)_{j\notin N_\ell}\), and \(\lvert N_\ell\rvert\le\overline d n^\alpha\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Zhao2026OptimalControlVariates, Assumption 1\(^{\dagger}\)(ii), directed dependency neighborhoods, \cite{Zhao2026OptimalControlVariates}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:zhao-first-order-rate]\label{ass:zhao-first-order-rate}
The nonnegative design-rate exponents satisfy \(4\alpha+4\beta<1\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Standard} (Zhao2026OptimalControlVariates, published \(4\alpha+4\beta<1\) variance-equivale, \cite{Zhao2026OptimalControlVariates}).
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:pilot-main-outcome-independence]\label{ass:pilot-main-outcome-independence}
The sigma-field generated by the two unpaired pilot samples is independent of the sigma-field generated jointly by \(W\) and the main potential-outcome array \(\{(Y_i(1),Y_i(0)):1\le i\le n\}\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Novel.} standard: independent external-pilot or sample-splitting condition
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[ass:coordinate-bounded-kappa-feasibility]\label{ass:coordinate-bounded-kappa-feasibility}
There exist \(b_1,b_0\in\mathbb R^n\) such that, for \(B=\begin{bmatrix}b_1&0\\0&b_0\end{bmatrix}\), \(\lVert b_1\rVert_2^2=\lVert b_0\rVert_2^2=n\), \(\max_{1\le i\le n,\,a\in\{1,0\}}|b_{a,i}|\le\overline a\), and \(\lambda_{\min}(n^{-1}B^{\top}\Omega B)\ge\kappa\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Novel.} novel: a finite-dimensional design-side feasibility condition checkable before outcomes from the known \(\Omega\), \(\kappa\), and \(\overline a\); existence is not attributed to Zhao.
\end{assumption}

\section{Definitions}

\begin{definition}[def:coupling-class]\label{def:coupling-class}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}Gamma(F\_\allowbreak{}1,\allowbreak{}F\_\allowbreak{}0)}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} \(\Gamma(F_1,F_0)=\{H\in\mathcal P([0,1]^2):\operatorname{marg}(H)=(F_1,F_0)\}\), the class carved by ass:fixed-marginals.
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Member properties.} \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}fixed-\allowbreak{}marginals}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:zhao-basis-class]\label{def:zhao-basis-class}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal B\_\allowbreak{}Z}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} \(\mathcal B_Z=\{[B]:B=\begin{bmatrix}b_1&0\\0&b_0\end{bmatrix},\ \lambda_{\min}(B^{\top}\Omega B)>0,\ b_1,b_0\in\mathbb R^n\}\), where \([B]\) quotients independent nonzero rescaling of the two columns and the predicates are ass:block-separable-basis and ass:basis-invertibility.
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Member properties.} \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}block-\allowbreak{}separable-\allowbreak{}basis}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}basis-\allowbreak{}invertibility}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:kappa-basis-class]\label{def:kappa-basis-class}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal B\_\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}kappa}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} \(\mathcal B_\kappa=\{[B]:B=\begin{bmatrix}b_1&0\\0&b_0\end{bmatrix},\ (\lVert b_1\rVert_2^2,\lVert b_0\rVert_2^2)=(n,n),\ \lambda_{\min}(n^{-1}B^{\top}\Omega B)\ge\kappa,\ b_1,b_0\in\mathbb R^n\}\), with the predicates ass:block-separable-basis, ass:projective-normalization, and ass:kappa-regularity.
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Member properties.} \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}block-\allowbreak{}separable-\allowbreak{}basis}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}projective-\allowbreak{}normalization}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}kappa-\allowbreak{}regularity}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:moment-matrix]\label{def:moment-matrix}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{Q(c)}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} For \(c\in\mathcal C(F_1,F_0)\), \(Q(c)=\begin{bmatrix}\mu_1^2\mathbf 1\mathbf 1^{\top}+\sigma_1^2I_n&-\mu_1\mu_0\mathbf 1\mathbf 1^{\top}-cI_n\\-\mu_1\mu_0\mathbf 1\mathbf 1^{\top}-cI_n&\mu_0^2\mathbf 1\mathbf 1^{\top}+\sigma_0^2I_n\end{bmatrix}\in\mathbb S_+^{2n}\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Inputs.} \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mu\_\allowbreak{}a}; \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}sigma\_\allowbreak{}a\textasciicircum{}2}; \texttt{c}; \texttt{n}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:gain-loss]\label{def:gain-loss}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{h(c),\allowbreak{}g\_\allowbreak{}B(c),\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}ell\_\allowbreak{}B(c)}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} For \(B\in\mathcal B_Z\) and \(c\in\mathcal C(F_1,F_0)\), define the uncontrolled ex ante variance \(h(c)=n^{-2}\operatorname{tr}[\Pi^{-1}\Omega\Pi^{-1}Q(c)]\), the control-variate gain \(g_B(c)=n^{-2}\operatorname{tr}[S_B^{-1}B^{\top}\Omega\Pi^{-1}Q(c)\Pi^{-1}\Omega B]\), and the residual variance \(\ell_B(c)=h(c)-g_B(c)\), all in \([0,\infty)\).
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Inputs.} \texttt{def:\allowbreak{}moment-\allowbreak{}matrix}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:lawful-instance-class]\label{def:lawful-instance-class}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathfrak X\_\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}kappa}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} \(\mathfrak X_\kappa=\{\xi=(\nu,F_1,F_0):\operatorname{ass{:}exposure\mbox{-}positivity}(\xi)\wedge\operatorname{ass{:}kappa\mbox{-}feasibility}(\xi)\wedge F_1,F_0\in\mathcal P([0,1])\}\) is carved by the single named positivity and feasibility predicates, with the relative topology inherited from total variation on the finite assignment simplex and the Wasserstein metric on each bounded marginal.
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Member properties.} \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}exposure-\allowbreak{}positivity}; \texttt{ass:\allowbreak{}kappa-\allowbreak{}feasibility}.
\end{definition}

\begin{definition}[def:zhao-rate-admissible-basis-class]\label{def:zhao-rate-admissible-basis-class}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Defined object.} \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal B\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}kappa,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}overline a\}\allowbreak{}}
\par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Construction.} For a fixed \(\overline a\in[1,\infty)\), define
\[
\mathcal B_{\kappa,\overline a}
=\left\{[B]\in\mathcal B_\kappa:
\max_{1\le i\le n,\,a\in\{1,0\}}|b_{a,i}|\le\overline a\right\}.
\]
This is the normalized Zhao-rate-admissible class.  The coordinate bound is imposed explicitly and is not inferred from \(\lVert b_a\rVert_2^2=n\).
